Let's design algorithm:

Option A: Direct optimization of normalized weights a length N (like 200 to 2000) using:

Let's formulate full plan for code:

```
import numpy as np, time, math
from scipy.optimize import minimize

def conv_full(a):
    # use rfft for speed; return float array length 2n-1
    n=len(a)
    L=1
    while L < 2*n-1: L <<=1
    A=np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    c=np.fft.irfft(A*A, L)[:2*n-1]
    c[np.abs(c)<1e-15]=0
    return c
```

But for gradient, if we use scipy minimize with black-box objective and analytic grad, need objective and gradient efficient. Let's implement custom class? Helper top-level.

For N up to maybe 512, direct np.convolve maybe simpler and exact enough. But FFT convenient.

Compute c and p for logsumexp:
```
def smooth_obj_grad(w, beta):
    a = softmax(w)
    c = conv(a)
    cx = np.clip(c,0,None)
    m = np.max(cx)
    e = np.exp(beta*(cx - m))
    Z = np.sum(e)
    p = e/Z
    lse = m + np.log(Z)
    val = 2*n*lse
    # h
    p_rev = p[::-1]
    # q = conv(a, p_rev)[2n-2 - i], need length 3n-3; use FFT
    Lq=1
    while Lq < 3*n-3: Lq <<=1
    ffta=np.fft.rfft(a, Lq)
    fftp=np.fft.rfft(p_rev, Lq)
    convq=np.fft.irfft(ffta*fftp, Lq)[:3*n-3]
    idx = 2*n-2 - np.arange(n)
    q=convq[idx]
    h=q
    diag_idx = 2*np.arange(n)
    h[diag_idx < len(p)] += p[diag_idx] * a
    total = np.dot(a, h)
    grad = 2*n * a * (h - total)
    return val, grad
```
Need sign? grad positive for ascent? Since val =2n lse, derivative wrt w calculated, yes. L-BFGS uses gradient to minimize.

Check softmax gradient: if a=softmax(w). d(a_i)/d(w_i)=a_i(1-δ), d(a_j)/d(w_i)=-a_i a_j. dF/dw_i = ∑_k dF/da_k d(a_k)/dw_i = a_i(F_a_i - ∑_j a_j F_a_j). Yes.

Potential issue: F_a=2n*h? LSE derivative wrt a = ∑ p_k ∂c_k/∂a (since dLSE/da = ∑ p_k ∂c_k/∂a). val=2n*LSE, so F_a=2n*h. total = ∑ a_j F_a_j. grad= a*(F_a - total). Good. We used grad=2n*a*(h - total_h) where total_h=∑ a h. Equivalent.

FFT convolution:
```
def convolve_fft(x, y):
    n = len(x)+len(y)-1
    L=1
    while L<n: L <<=1
    X=np.fft.rfft(x,L); Y=np.fft.rfft(y,L)
    return np.fft.irfft(X*Y,L)[:n]
```
For c, y=x. Roundoff ~1e-16. Clip small negative.

Let's write detailed helpers.

`softmax(w)`:
```
def softmax(x):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
    x = x - np.max(x)
    e = np.exp(x)
    s = e.sum()
    if s <=0 or not np.isfinite(s):
       return np.full_like(x, 1.0/len(x))
    return e/s
```

`convolve_full(x)`:
Use direct for len <= maybe 300? Actually direct `np.convolve(x,x)` returns length 2n-1, C optimized. For gradient q direct maybe use correlate; but for c, for n<=512 direct exact and fast. For n>512 FFT. Let's implement FFT always to avoid O(n²), but direct may be more accurate. FFT roundoff can make max slightly above true; if used for search okay. Final exact. For n small, FFT may have tiny imaginary; clip. Use nextfast? Could use `scipy.fft.next_fast_len` but maybe not. We'll do power of two.

```
def convolve_full(x):
    n=len(x)
    if n <= 300:
       return np.convolve(x,x)
    need=2*n-1
    L=1
    while L<need: L <<=1
    X=np.fft.rfft(x,L)
    c=np.fft.irfft(X*X, L)[:need]
    c[c<0 & c>-1e-12]=0
    return c
```
FFT of length 2048 for n=1024 okay. For n=2048 L=4096. Good.

Gradient q needs p length 2n-1. Use FFT. Could reuse c FFT? c coefficients are inverse of A². p is normalized exp of c; no.

`objective_and_grad(w)`:
```
def objective_and_grad(w):
    w=np.asarray(w,dtype=np.float64)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(w)):
        return (1e100, np.zeros_like(w))
    w = w - np.mean(w)
    a=softmax(w)
    n=len(a)
    # If w has huge values, softmax okay.
    c=convolve_full(a)
    c=np.asarray(c,dtype=np.float64)
    if c.size != 2*n-1:
       c=np.convolve(a,a)
    c=c - np.max(c) # stabilized; but p relative only. Use c0 = np.clip(c_cpu)
    cmax=np.max(c)
    c_clip=np.clip(c,0.0,None)
    beta=_GLOBAL_BETA
    with np.errstate(over='ignore', under='ignore'):
        e=np.exp(beta*(c_clip-cmax))
    Z=e.sum()
    if not np.isfinite(Z) or Z<=0:
        p=np.zeros_like(c); p[np.argmax(c_clip)]=1.0
    else:
        p=e/Z
    lse = cmax + np.log(Z) if Z>0 else cmax
    val = 2.0*n*lse + 1e-12*np.dot(w,w)
    # gradient high level
    # compute h
    p_rev = p[::-1]
    L=1
    while L < 3*n - 3:
        L <<=1
    AF=np.fft.rfft(a,L)
    PF=np.fft.rfft(p_rev,L)
    convq=np.fft.irfft(AF*PF, L)
    convq = convq[:3*n-3]
    idx = _GLOBAL_IDX ? # Need compute each call? arange n each time small.
    q=convq[2*n-2 - np.arange(n)]
    h=q
    di=2*np.arange(n)
    h[di < len(p)] += p[di] * a
    total=float(np.dot(a,h))
    grad = 2.0*n * a * (h - total)
    grad += 2e-12 * w
    return val, grad
```
But c Clip: We subtract cmax; for p only relative. lse = cmax + log Z. If c has tiny negative due FFT, cmax maybe max. Clipping to 0 changes lse very slightly; okay. For gradient, p based on clipped. If true c has tiny negatives, no effect.

Potential issue: If beta very high, e all underflow except one, Z might be 0 if max is negative? But e at cmax =1, so Z≥1. Good.

Need `_GLOBAL_BETA` maybe float.

L-BFGS may evaluate gradient many times; computing `np.arange(n)` each call okay. Could precompute but no closure. For n changes, local arrays.

`optimize_w(w0, N, max_time, starts?)`:

```

L-BFGS-B `maxiter` counts iterations, for nonconvex can be slow. Use smaller. We can track best w by exact/smooth score after each stage.

```
def try_lbgfs(w, N, time_limit, use_betas=True):
    global _GLOBAL_DEADLINE, _GLOBAL_BETA
    deadline = min(time.monotonic()+time_limit, _GLOBAL_DEADLINE)
    w = np.asarray(w,...).copy(); w-=w.mean()
    best_w=w; best_score=fast_score_norm(softmax(w))
    stages=[10,50,200,1000,5000, max(10000,50*N)]
    for beta in stages:
       if time.monotonic()>deadline: break
       _GLOBAL_BETA=float(beta)
       res = minimize(objective_and_grad, w, method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True, callback=_deadline_callback, options={'maxiter':80, 'ftol':1e-8, 'gtol':1e-5, 'maxls':30, 'disp':False})
       if res.x is finite: w=res.x
       a=softmax(w); sc=fast_score_norm(a); if sc<best: update
    return best_w, best_score
```

Initializations:
```
def linear_init(N, slope):
    x = np.linspace(0.0,1.0,N)
    f = 1.0 + slope*x
    if slope< -0.99: f=...; ensure positive: f=np.clip(f,1e-8, None)
    f = f / f.sum()
    return np.log(f)

def family_inits(N, rng):
    inits=[]
    for slope in [-1.5,-1,-0.5,0.5,1,1.2,1.5,2]:
       inits.append(linear_init(N,slope))
    # quadratic through t: f = 1 + a*t + b*t*(t-1)
    for a in [-2,-1,0,1,2,3]:
       for b in [-2,-1,0,1,2]:
           f = 1 + a*x + b*x*(x-1)
           if f.min() > 1e-8:
              inits.append(np.log(f/f.sum()))
    # log-polynomial random
    for _ in range(4):
       coeffs = rng.normal(0,1,size=4)
       f = np.exp(coeffs[0]+coeffs[1]*x+coeffs[2]*x**2+coeffs[3]*x**3)
       if f.min()>1e-8: inits.append(np.log(f/f.sum()))
    # random Dirichlet
    for alpha in [0.2,0.5,1,2]:
       f = rng.dirichlet(np.full(N, alpha))
       inits.append(np.log(f+1e-300))
    return inits
```

Resize function:
```
def resize_sequence(a, N):
    n=len(a)
    if n==N: return a.copy()
    if n==0: return np.ones(N)/N
    x_old=np.linspace(0,1,n); x_new=np.linspace(0,1,N)
    return np.interp(x_new,x_old,a)
```

Active-set exponentiated gradient as polishing:
```
def active_polish(a, time_limit, rng):
   n=len(a); w=log(a); last=score
   steps=...
   beta=...
   for iter:
      c=conv; maxc
      # p = softmax(beta*c) with beta high
      p = ...
      h = gradient_part
      total = a*h
      grad = a*(h-total)
      # adaptive step: try lr list and pick best score
      best=a
      for lr in [1e-1,5e-2,2e-2,1e-2,5e-3]:
          w_new = w - lr*grad
          a_new=softmax(w_new); sc=score; if better
      if no improvement break or decay lr
      w=log(best)
```

Detailed helper functions:

```
_GLOBAL_DEADLINE = 0.0
_LOCAL_DEADLINE = 0.1
_GLOBAL_BETA = 1.0

def _deadline_callback(xk):
    return time.monotonic() > _LOCAL_DEADLINE
```
No closure.

`fast_score(a)`:
```
def fast_score(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size==0: return np.inf
    s=a.sum()
    if s <= 0.01: return np.inf # But for search normalized s=1
    a = np.clip(a,0.0,1000.0)
    s=np.sum(a)
    c=np.convolve_fft(a) # or direct
    m=np.max(c)
    if m < 0 and m > -1e-12: m=0
    return float(2*a.size*m/(s*s))
```
Our search normalized, but could use unnormalized.

`convolve_fft` as above.

For `objective_and_grad`, use normalized `_best_n`? It infers n. Need ensure `beta` not zero. If beta=0? p uniform? We start beta=10. Good.

Potential memory: FFT for N=2048 Lc=4096, q L=8192. Fine.

`optimize_w`:
```
def optimize_w(w, time_limit):
    global _LOCAL_DEADLINE, _GLOBAL_BETA
    start=time.monotonic()
    deadline=min(start+time_limit, _GLOBAL_DEADLINE)
    _LOCAL_DEADLINE=deadline
    # maybe ensure w finite and centered
    w = np.asarray(w,dtype=float).copy()
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(w)): return None
    w -= np.mean(w)
    best_w=w.copy()
    best_a=softmax(w); best_sc=fast_score(best_a)
    if best_sc < best_global? not inside
    n=len(w)
    betas = [10, 50, 200, 1000, 5000, max(10000, 50*n)]
    # remove duplicates, cap maybe <= 200000
    for beta in betas:
       if time.monotonic() > deadline - 2: break
       _GLOBAL_BETA=float(beta)
       try:
          res = minimize(objective_and_grad, w, method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True, callback=_deadline_callback,
                         options={'maxiter': 60, 'ftol':1e-7, 'gtol':1e-4, 'maxls':20, 'disp':False})
       except Exception:
          break
       if res.x is not None and np.all(np.isfinite(res.x)):
          w=res.x - np.mean(res.x)
       a=softmax(w); sc=fast_score(a)
       if sc < best_sc:
          best_sc=sc; best_w=w
       # if sc improved little? continue
    return best_w, best_sc
```

Local search implementation:
```
def local_search(a, rng, time_limit):
    n=len(a); a=a.copy(); a=np.maximum(a,0); s=a.sum(); if s<1e-9: return None
    a=a/s
    best=a.copy(); sc=fast_score(best)
    deadline=time.monotonic()+time_limit
    sigma=0.15
    step=0
    while time.monotonic()<deadline:
       cand=best.copy()
       # choose width
       if n>8:
          lo=max(1,n//200); hi=max(2,n//12)
          width=int(rng.integers(lo,hi+1))
          start=int(rng.integers(0,n-width+1))
          factor=float(np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, sigma)))
          cand[start:start+width]*=factor
       else:
          idx=int(rng.integers(0,n)); cand[idx]*=float(np.exp(rng.normal(0,sigma)))
       # occasional transfer
       if n>2 and rng.random()<0.3:
          i=int(rng.integers(0,n)); j=int(rng.integers(0,n))
          if i!=j:
             delta = float(rng.normal(0.0, max(1e-6, 0.05*best[i])))
             # keep i nonnegative
             newi=best[i]+delta
             if newi>0:
                cand[j]=cand[j]-delta
                # if cand[j] negative? use scaling instead
       # simpler: multiplicative perturbation with random block and maybe random global tilt
       if rng.random()<0.2:
           t=np.linspace(0,1,n)
           tilt=float(rng.normal(0,0.5))
           cand *= np.exp(tilt*t)
       cand=np.maximum(cand,0.0)
       s=cand.sum()
       if s<=0: continue
       cand/=s
       sc=fast_score(cand)
       if sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
           best=cand; best_sc=sc
           # reduce sigma slightly when improve
           sigma=max(0.01, sigma*0.99)
       else:
           # annealing accept? maybe rare
           pass
    return best, best_sc
```
But random block multiplicative may not adjust global shape well. Add "tilt" perturbations: multiply by exp(β*t) with random β, which changes skew. Good.

We can also use active gradient polish in local search:
```
def gradient_polish(a, rng, time_limit):
    w=np.log(a+1e-300); last=fast_score(a); lr=0.05
    for iter in range(max_iters):
       beta_high = max(1000, 20*n)
       _GLOBAL_BETA=beta_high
       val,grad=objective_and_grad(w)  # uses _GLOBAL_BETA high
       # line search lr values
       improved=False
       for step in [1e-4,3e-4,1e-3,3e-3,1e-2,3e-2]:
           w_new=w - step*grad
           a_new=softmax(w_new); sc=fast_score(a_new)
           if sc<last:
               w=w_new; a=a_new; last=sc; improved=True; break
       if not improved:
           lr*=0.7; if lr<1e-6: break
       if time over break
    return a,last
```
This is similar to L-BFGS but simple; maybe less efficient. Could use after final.

Good.

Now, mathematical check of gradient for smooth objective: The objective val includes `+1e-12 dot(w,w)`. gradient includes `2e-12 w`. In `objective_and_grad`, we center w: `w_centered = np.asarray(w)-mean`. Softmax on centered. But gradient returned should correspond to original w input. If we compute a from centered, gradient wrt centered w_s: g_s = 2n a(h - total) + 2e-12 w_s. Relationship to original w: w_s = w - mean(w) (model matrix M = I - (1/n)J). a depends on w_s. da/dw = da/dw_s * M. Since g_s is in range M^T? g_s sum zero? g_s first term sum zero, plus 2e-12 w_s may not sum zero? w_s sum zero, so g_s sum zero. For sum-zero g_s, g_s = g_s M? Because M acts identity on sum-zero. Gradient wrt original w = M^T g_s = M g_s (since symmetric) = g_s. So okay. If we return g_s, fine. But in L-BFGS using input w, if w has mean, gradient sum zero, it won't reduce mean. Internal centering may make function flat in mean; okay.

def density_to_w(f):

   f=np.where(np.isfinite(f), f, 0.0)

   f=f/s

`resize_a`:
```
def resize_a(a, N):
    a=np.asarray(a); n=a.size
    if N==n: return a.copy()
    if n==1: return np.full(N,a[0]/N?) # normalize later
    x_old=np.linspace(0,1,n); x_new=np.linspace(0,1,N)
    a_new=np.interp(x_new,x_old,a)
    a_new=np.maximum(a_new,1e-300)
    s=a_new.sum(); return a_new/s
```

def gradient_polish(a, time_limit):
    n=len(a); a=normalize
    w=np.log(a+1e-300); w-=w.mean()
    best=a.copy(); best_sc=fast_score(a)
    deadline=time.monotonic()+time_limit
    lr=0.05
    stall=0
    while time.monotonic()<deadline and lr>1e-6:
       _GLOBAL_BETA = max(5000, 30*n)
       try: val,grad = objective_and_grad(w)
       except: break
       if not finite grad: break
       # direction is -grad. Try steps
       improved=False
       base_sc=fast_score(softmax(w)) if needed? use best_sc for current? Need compare to current a score; we can keep curr_sc.
       for step in [lr, lr*0.5, lr*0.2, 1e-3, 5e-4]:
           w_trial=w - step*grad
           a_trial=softmax(w_trial)
           sc=fast_score(a_trial)
           if sc < curr_sc - 1e-12:
               w=w_trial; curr=a_trial; curr_sc=sc; improved=True; break
       if improved:
           if curr_sc < best_sc: best=curr; best_sc=curr_sc
           stall=0
           lr=min(0.5, lr*1.1?) maybe keep
       else:
           stall+=1; lr*=0.6
           if stall>5: break
    return best,best_sc
```

Now, possible numeric issue with `w` large >1e300? softmax subtract max. If w has infinite, check. Initial log(1e-300) ~ -690, finite. L-BFGS might push to -1000, still fine; exp of max-subzero =1, others underflow. Good.

Let's write `softmx`:
```
def softmax(x):
    x=np.asarray(x,dtype=np.float64).copy()
    m=np.max(x)
    if not np.isfinite(m):
       x=np.where(np.isfinite(x), x, 0.0); m=np.max(x)
    x=x-m
    e=np.exp(x)
    s=e.sum()
    if s<=0 or not np.isfinite(s):
       return np.full(x.shape, 1.0/x.size)
    return e/s
```

`density_to_w`: if f small, log; if f sum huge? values maybe exp polynomial with overflow; use safe: `f=np.logaddexp`? We can compute log density directly to avoid overflow. For polynomial log starts, better create w directly. But density_to_w can handle if f finite; `np.exp(c...)` may overflow for random coeffs magnitude> maybe. We can scale log: `logf = c0 + ...`; subtract max before exp.
Define top-level `logpoly_to_w(coeffs,N)` maybe. Simpler in family: compute `logf = ...; m=np.max(logf); w=logf-m` then softmax? For density w, any shift irrelevant; we can return `logf - np.mean(logf)`? Since softmax(w) normalizes. For linear, log(1+B x) finite. For random polynomial, do logf.

In `family_starts`, for log-polynomial:
```
c=rng.normal(0,2,4)
logf=c[0]+c[1]*x+c[2]*x**2+c[3]*x**3
logf-=np.max(logf)
w=logf - np.mean(logf)
```
This ensures finite. For trig with product, can log if positive.

For Dirichlet, use log.

So `density_to_w` only for positive f.

Now, family starts code with no nested. Let's outline:
```
def family_starts(N, rng):
    starts=[]
    x=np.linspace(0,1,N)
    for B in ...:
       f=1.0+B*x
       if np.min(f) > 1e-8:
          w=density_to_w(f); if w is not None: starts.append(w)
       else:
          # clipped sparse?
          w=density_to_w(f)
          if w is not None: starts.append(w)
    for B in ...:
      for C in ...:
         f=1.0+B*x+C*x*(x-1.0)
         w=density_to_w(f); if w is not None: starts.append(w)
    for _ in range(12):
      c=rng.normal(0.0,1.0,4)
      logf=c[0]+c[1]*x+c[2]*x*x+c[3]*x*x*x
      logf=logf-np.max(logf)
      starts.append(logf - np.mean(logf))
    for _ in range(10):
      amp1=rng.uniform(-1,1); amp2=rng.uniform(-1,1); base=rng.uniform(0,1.5); phase=rng.uniform(0,1)
      shape=1.0 + amp1*np.sin(np.pi*x+phase) + amp2*np.sin(2*np.pi*x+phase)
      f=(1.0+base*x)*shape
      w=density_to_w(f); if w is not None: starts.append(w)
    for alpha in (0.1,0.25,0.5,1.0,2.0):
      f=rng.dirichlet(np.full(N, alpha))
      w=density_to_w(f); if w is not None: starts.append(w)
    # sparse
    for frac in (0.05,0.1,0.2):
       m=max(2,int(N*frac))
       f=np.full(N,1e-10)
       pos=rng.choice(N,size=m,replace=False)
       f[pos]=rng.exponential(1.0)
       w=density_to_w(f); if w is not None: starts.append(w)
    # limit
    if len(starts)>80: starts=starts[:80]
    return starts
```
`np.full(N, alpha)` no lambda.

Maybe `density_to_w` with f full of 1e-10 and exponential: okay.

Now, local_search random details:
```
def local_search(a, rng, time_limit):
    a=np.asarray(a,float).copy()
    a=np.clip(a,0,None); s=a.sum()
    if s <=0: return a, np.inf
    a/=s
    n=a.size
    best=a.copy(); best_sc=fast_score(a)
    deadline=time.monotonic()+time_limit
    sigma=0.2
    it=0
    while time.monotonic()<deadline:
       cand=best.copy()
       r=rng.random()
       if r <0.45:
          # block
          lo=max(1, n//300) if n>10 else 1
          hi=max(lo+1, n//10)
          if hi>=n: hi=n-1
          if hi>lo:
             width=int(rng.integers(lo, hi+1))
             start=int(rng.integers(0, n-width+1))
             factor=float(np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, sigma)))
             cand[start:start+width]*=factor
       elif r<0.6:
          # tilt
          slope=float(rng.normal(0.0,0.8))
          t=np.linspace(0,1,n)
          cand *= np.exp(slope*t)
       elif r<0.75:
          # smooth wavy
          t=np.linspace(0,1,n)
          amp=float(rng.normal(0.0,0.3))
          freq=float(rng.uniform(1,4))
          phase=float(rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi))
          cand *= np.exp(amp*np.sin(freq*np.pi*t+phase))
       else:
          # pair transfer
          i=int(rng.integers(0,n)); j=int(rng.integers(0,n))
          if i!=j:
             frac=float(rng.uniform(-0.5,0.5))
             move=frac*best[i]
             if best[i]-move >0:
                cand[i]-=move
                cand[j]+=move
       cand=np.maximum(cand,0.0)
       s=cand.sum()
       if s<=0: continue
       cand/=s
       sc=fast_score(cand)
       if sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
          best=cand; best_sc=sc
          sigma=max(0.02, sigma*0.995)
       # optional rare worse acceptance
       elif rng.random() < 0.002 and sc < best_sc + 1e-5:
          best=cand; best_sc=sc
       it+=1
    return best,best_sc
```
No lambda.

`gradient_polish` maybe skip to reduce. But we can include simple after local if time. Let's include but make conservative.

```
def gradient_polish(a, time_limit):
    n=a.size; a=normalize; w=np.log(a+1e-300); w-=w.mean()
    best=a.copy(); best_sc=fast_score(a); curr_sc=best_sc
    deadline=...
    lr=0.05; stall=0
    while time.monotonic()<deadline and lr>1e-7:
       _GLOBAL_BETA = max(5000.0, 30.0*n)
       try: val,grad=objective_and_grad(w)
       except Exception: break
       if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)): break
       improved=False
       for step in (lr, lr*0.5, lr*0.2, 1e-3,5e-4,1e-4):
           w_trial=w - step*grad
           a_trial=softmax(w_trial)
           sc=fast_score(a_trial)
           if sc < curr_sc - 1e-13:
              w=w_trial; curr=a_trial; curr_sc=sc; improved=True; break
       if improved:
          if curr_sc < best_sc: best=curr; best_sc=curr_sc
          stall=0
          lr=min(0.2, lr*1.05)
       else:
          stall+=1; lr*=0.6
          if stall>6: break
    return best,best_sc
```
Need global `_GLOBAL_BETA` set. This may be slow due many FFT. Time_limit small e.g. 30s.

Potential issue: gradient `grad` from objective_and_grad includes tiny L2 penalty wrt `w` centered; if w has large values, line search may move weird. okay.

def propose_candidate(seed=42,budget_s=2400,**kwargs):
    global _GLOBAL_DEADLINE
    start=time.monotonic()
    budget=float(budget_s) if budget_s is not None else 2400
    _GLOBAL_DEADLINE=start+max(1,budget-6)
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed if seed is not None else 42)
    best_seq=None; best_exact=np.inf; best_fast_seq=None; best_fast=np.inf; pool=[]
    base_a=None
    hs=globals().get('height_sequence_1')
    if hs is not None:
       arr=np.asarray(hs,dtype=float)
       if arr.ndim==1 and arr.size>0 and np.all(np.isfinite(arr)):
          arr=np.clip(arr,0,1000)
          if arr.sum()>0.01:
             arr=arr/arr.sum()
             base_a=arr
             consider with do_exact True? maybe
    # quick family
    quick_Ns = [8,16,32,64,128,256,512]
    for N in quick_Ns:
       if time.monotonic() > _GLOBAL_DEADLINE-60: break
       for w in family_starts(N,rng):
          a=softmax(w)
          sc=fast_score(a)
          if sc < best_fast:

          if sc < 2.0 - 1e-9:
             pool=add_to_pool(pool,a,sc,6)
    # optimize plans
    plans = [(32,3,30), (64,4,40), (128,5,60), (256,5,90), (512,4,100), (1024,3,90)]
    for N,num,sec in plans:
       if time.monotonic() > _GLOBAL_DEADLINE-30: break
       allowed_sec = max(5, min(sec, max(0, _GLOBAL_DEADLINE - time.monotonic() - 60)/max(1,len(remaining_plans))))? Simpler if time left.

       if base_a is not None:

       # score starts and top
       scored=[]

          if sc<best_fast: update
          scored.append((sc,w,a))
       scored.sort()
       top = scored[:num] if scored else []
       if not top: 
          # use best_fast resized
          if best_fast_seq is not None:
             a=resize_a(best_fast_seq,N); w=density_to_w(a); top=[(best_fast,w,a)]
       for sc,w,a in top:
          if time.monotonic() > _GLOBAL_DEADLINE-20: break
          # allocate time per start: min(sec, remaining/num?)
          tleft=_GLOBAL_DEADLINE-time.monotonic()-30
          tper=max(5, min(sec, tleft/(num+1)))
          w2, sc2 = optimize_w(w, tper)

             a2=softmax(w2)
             # maybe gradient polish for a small part
             pol_chan = min(15, (tper*0.3))

                 a3,sc3=gradient_polish(a2,pol_chan)
                 if sc3<sc2: a2,sc2=a3,sc3
             # local short
             loc_t = max(5, min(tper*0.5, tleft/...??))
             # Simpler: local after all top? We'll do a small local after each candidate 5-20s
             a_loc, sc_loc = local_search(a2,rng,min(20, tleft/2))
             if sc_loc < sc2: a2,sc2=a_loc,sc_loc
             # consider exact and pool
             best_seq,best_exact,best_fast_seq,best_fast=consider_candidate(a2,best_seq,best_exact,best_fast_seq,best_fast,do_exact=True)
             if sc_loc < best_fast+0.02: pool=add_to_pool(pool,a2,sc_loc,6)
       # end for
    # If best score not good and time remains, try large N from best
    if time.monotonic() < _GLOBAL_DEADLINE-100 and best_fast_seq is not None:
       for N in [1536,2048]:
          if time over break
          if best_fast_seq.size != N:
             a0=resize_a(best_fast_seq,N); w0=density_to_w(a0)
          else: a0=...
          if w0 is None: continue
          tper=min(150, _GLOBAL_DEADLINE-time.monotonic()-60)
          w2,sc2=optimize_w(w0,tper)
          if w2 is not None:
             a2=softmax(w2)
             a2,sc2=local_search(a2,rng,min(60, tleft/2))
             consider exact; pool
    # final local on pool top 3
    if len(pool)>0:
       top=pool[:3]
       remaining=_GLOBAL_DEADLINE-time.monotonic()-10
       if remaining>10:
          tper=remaining/len(top)
          for sc,a in top:
             if time over break
             a2,sc2=local_search(a,rng,tper)
             # gradient polish maybe
             if sc2<sc:
                pool.add
             consider exact
    # final choose
    if best_seq is None and best_fast_seq is not None:
       # evaluate exact best_fast
       try: sc=evaluate_sequence(best_fast_seq.tolist()); if sc<best_exact: best_seq=best_fast_seq; best_exact=sc
       except: pass
    if best_seq is None and base_a is not None: best_seq=base_a
    if best_seq is None: best_seq=np.ones(64)
    return [float(max(0.0,min(1000.0,x))) for x in best_seq]
```

Let's implement `optimize_w` robust:
```
def optimize_w(w0, time_limit):
    global _LOCAL_DEADLINE, _GLOBAL_BETA
    w=np.asarray(w0,dtype=float).copy()
    if w.size==0 or not finite: return None, np.inf
    w-=np.mean(w)
    a=softmax(w); best_w=w.copy(); best_sc=fast_score(a)
    if not _HAVE_SCIPY: return best_w,best_sc
    start=time.monotonic()
    local_deadline=min(start+max(1.0,time_limit), _GLOBAL_DEADLINE)
    _LOCAL_DEADLINE=local_deadline
    n=w.size
    # betas
    max_beta=max(20000.0, 40.0*n)
    betas=[20.0,100.0,500.0,2000.0,10000.0,max_beta]
    # filter unique increasing within reason
    uniq=[]
    for b in betas:
       if b>uniq and (not uniq or b > uniq[-1]*2): uniq.append(b)
    for beta in uniq:
       if time.monotonic() > local_deadline - 3: break
       _GLOBAL_BETA=beta
       maxit = 80 if n<=64 else 60 if n<=256 else 40 if n<=1024 else 30
       try:
          res = minimize(objective_and_grad, w, method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True, callback=_deadline_callback,
                         options={'maxiter': maxit, 'ftol':1e-6, 'gtol':1e-3, 'maxls':12, 'disp':False})
       except Exception:
          break
       if res.x is not None and np.all(np.isfinite(res.x)):
          w=res.x - np.mean(res.x)
       a=softmax(w); sc=fast_score(a)
       if sc < best_sc:
          best_sc=sc; best_w=w.copy()
    return best_w,best_sc
```
We don't know if line search with high beta and non-smooth may produce warnings; okay.

def consider_candidate(a, best_seq, best_exact, best_fast_seq, best_fast, do_exact=False):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == 0: return ...
    a=np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a=np.clip(a,0.0,1000.0)
    s=a.sum()
    if s <=0.01:
       if not best_seq? return same
    a=a/s
    sc_f=fast_score(a)
    if not np.isfinite(sc_f): return same
    if sc_f < best_fast:
       best_fast=sc_f; best_fast_seq=a.copy()
    if do_exact and a.size <= 4000:
       try:
          sc_e=float(evaluate_sequence(a.tolist()))
       except Exception:
          sc_e=np.inf
       if np.isfinite(sc_e) and sc_e < best_exact:
          best_exact=sc_e; best_seq=a.copy()
    return best_seq,best_exact,best_fast_seq,best_fast
```

`add_to_pool` top-level:
```
def add_to_pool(pool,a,score,max_size=6):
    if a is None or score is None or not np.isfinite(score): return pool
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=float)
    if a.size==0 or not np.all(np.isfinite(a)): return pool
    pool = [(float(s), b.copy()) for s,b in pool]
    pool.append((float(score), a.copy()))
    pool.sort()
    if len(pool)>max_size:
       pool=pool[:max_size]
    return pool
```

In `fast_score`, if a size large and sum=1, conv via FFT. For exact/N direct? Use `convolve_fft`.

```
def convolve_fft(x):
    n=x.size
    if n == 1:
        return np.array([x[0]*x[0]])
    if n <= 256:
        return np.convolve(x,x)
    need=2*n-1
    L=1
    while L<need:
        L <<=1
    X=np.fft.rfft(x,L)
    c=np.fft.irfft(X*X, L)[:need]
    # Remove tiny imaginary? irfft real. Clip negatives small.
    c[np.abs(c) < 1e-14] = 0.0
    return c
```
If c has negative -1e-8 due round? Maybe not. Use `c = np.maximum(c,0.0)`? But negative true impossible; clip all negatives to0. Could bias but okay. `c = np.where(c < 0, 0.0, c)` in fast. In objective, use clip.

`fast_score`:
```
def fast_score(a):
   a=np.asarray(a,float)
   if a.size==0: return np.inf
   a=np.where(np.isfinite(a),a,0.0)
   a=np.clip(a,0,1000)
   s=a.sum()
   if s <=1e-12: return np.inf
   c=convolve_fft(a)
   c=np.where(c>0,c,0)
   m=float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
   return float(2*a.size*m/(s*s))
```
For normalized, s=1.

`density_to_w`:
```
def density_to_w(f):
    f=np.asarray(f,float)
    if f.size==0: return None
    f=np.where(np.isfinite(f), f, 0.0)
    f=np.maximum(f, 1e-300)
    s=f.sum()
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300: return None
    f=f/s
    # Avoid extremely small causing finite w
    f=np.maximum(f, 1e-300)
    w=np.log(f)
    w-=np.mean(w)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(w)): return None
    return w
```
If some f clipped to1e-300 and others large, w range 700, okay.

```
def objective_and_grad(w):
    w=np.asarray(w,float)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(w)):
        val=float('inf'); grad=np.zeros(w.shape); return val, grad
    w = w - np.mean(w)
    a=softmax(w)
    n=a.size
    c=convolve_full? use convolve_fft and clip
    c=np.asarray(c,float)
    c=np.where(c>0.0,c,0.0)
    cmax=float(np.max(c))
    beta=float(_GLOBAL_BETA)
    if beta <=0: beta=1e-8
    with np.errstate(over='ignore', under='ignore', invalid='ignore'):
        e=np.exp(beta*(c-cmax))
    e=np.where(np.isfinite(e), e, 0.0)
    Z=float(np.sum(e))
    if Z <=0 or not np.isfinite(Z):
        p=np.zeros(c.shape); p[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
    else:
        p=e/Z
    lse=cmax + np.log(Z)
    val=2.0*n*lse + 1e-12*float(np.dot(w,w))
    # q
    p_rev=p[::-1]
    need=3*n-3
    if need <=0:
       convq=np.array([...])? n=1 -> conv length1.
    else:
       L=1
       while L < max(1, need): L <<=1
       AF=np.fft.rfft(a,L)
       PF=np.fft.rfft(p_rev,L)
       convq=np.fft.irfft(AF*PF,L)
       if convq.size < 3*n-3: convq=np.pad(convq, (0, (3*n-3)-convq.size))
       convq=convq[:max(1,3*n-3)]
    idx = 2*n-2 - np.arange(n)
    q = convq[idx] if q? For n=1 convq length at least1.
    q=np.where(q>0.0,q,0.0)
    h=2.0*q
    total=float(np.dot(a,h))
    grad=2.0*n * a * (h - total)
    grad += 2e-12 * w
    return val, grad
```

def gradient_polish(a, time_limit):
    a=np.asarray(a,float); normalize
    w=np.log(a+1e-300); w-=w.mean()
    best=a.copy(); best_sc=fast_score(a); curr_sc=best_sc
    deadline=time.monotonic()+max(1.0,time_limit)
    global _GLOBAL_BETA

```python
import numpy as np
import time

try:
    from scipy.optimize import minimize
    _HAVE_SCIPY = True
except Exception:
    _HAVE_SCIPY = False

_GLOBAL_DEADLINE = 0.0
_LOCAL_DEADLINE = 0.0
_GLOBAL_BETA = 1.0
_POOL_COUNTER = 0


def _deadline_callback(xk):
    return time.monotonic() > _LOCAL_DEADLINE


def softmax(x):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    x = np.where(np.isfinite(x), x, 0.0)
    m = float(np.max(x))
    if not np.isfinite(m):
        return np.full(x.shape, 1.0 / max(1, x.size), dtype=np.float64)
    x = x - m
    e = np.exp(x)
    s = float(np.sum(e))
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.full(x.shape, 1.0 / max(1, x.size), dtype=np.float64)
    return e / s


def convolve_fft(x):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
    n = x.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    if n == 1:
        return np.array([x[0] * x[0]], dtype=np.float64)
    if n <= 256:
        return np.convolve(x, x).astype(np.float64)
    need = 2 * n - 1
    L = 1
    while L < need:
        L <<= 1
    X = np.fft.rfft(x, L)
    c = np.fft.irfft(X * X, L)[:need]
    c = c.astype(np.float64, copy=False)
    c[c < 0.0] = 0.0
    return c


def fast_score(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if s <= 1e-12:
        return np.inf
    c = convolve_fft(a)
    if c.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    c = np.where(c > 0.0, c, 0.0)
    m = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(m):
        return np.inf
    return float(2.0 * a.size * m / (s * s))


def density_to_w(f):
    f = np.asarray(f, dtype=np.float64)
    if f.size == 0:
        return None
    f = np.where(np.isfinite(f), f, 0.0)
    f = np.maximum(f, 1e-300)
    s = float(np.sum(f))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return None
    f = f / s
    f = np.maximum(f, 1e-300)
    w = np.log(f)
    w = w - float(np.mean(w))
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(w)):
        return None
    return w


def resize_a(a, N):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.full(N, 1.0 / max(1, N), dtype=np.float64)
    if N == n:
        a = np.maximum(a, 1e-300)
        s = a.sum()
        if s <= 0.0:
            a = np.full(N, 1.0 / max(1, N), dtype=np.float64)
        else:
            a = a / s
        return a
    if n == 1:
        return np.full(N, 1.0 / N, dtype=np.float64)
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, N)
    a_new = np.interp(x_new, x_old, np.maximum(a, 0.0))
    a_new = np.maximum(a_new, 1e-300)
    s = float(a_new.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.full(N, 1.0 / max(1, N), dtype=np.float64)
    return a_new / s


def family_starts(N, rng):
    starts = []
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, N)
    xe = np.maximum(x, 1e-12)
    one_minus = np.maximum(1.0 - x, 1e-12)

    # Linear tilts.  The best simple two-point behavior is asymmetric, so this
    # family is an important seed.
    for B in (-2.0, -1.5, -1.0, -0.6, -0.2, 0.0,
              0.4, 0.7, 0.9, 1.0, 1.2, 1.4, 1.5, 1.7, 2.0, 2.5):
        f = 1.0 + B * x
        w = density_to_w(f)
        if w is not None:
            starts.append(w)

    # Low-order quadratic seeds.  The term x*(1-x) allows endpoint skew while
    # adjusting curvature.
    for B in (-1.5, -1.0, -0.5, 0.0, 0.5, 0.8, 1.0, 1.4, 2.0):
        for C in (-2.0, -1.2, -0.6, -0.3, 0.0, 0.3, 0.6, 1.2, 2.0):
            f = 1.0 + B * x + C * x * (x - 1.0)
            w = density_to_w(f)
            if w is not None:
                starts.append(w)

    # Random log-polynomial seeds.
    for _ in range(12):
        c = rng.normal(0.0, 1.5, 4)
        logf = c[0] + c[1] * x + c[2] * x * x + c[3] * x * x * x
        logf = logf - float(np.max(logf))
        w = logf - float(np.mean(logf))
        if np.all(np.isfinite(w)):
            starts.append(w)

    # Random symmetric/smooth seeds.
    for _ in range(12):
        a1 = rng.uniform(-1.0, 1.0)
        a2 = rng.uniform(-1.0, 1.0)
        base = rng.uniform(0.0, 1.5)
        phase = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi)
        shape = 1.0 + a1 * np.sin(np.pi * x + phase) + a2 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * x + phase)
        f = (1.0 + base * x) * shape
        w = density_to_w(f)
        if w is not None:
            starts.append(w)

    # Beta-like seeds, including endpoints that vanish.
    for p in (0.2, 0.5, 1.0, 1.5, 2.0, 3.0):
        for q in (0.0, 0.2, 0.5, 1.0, 2.0):
            logf = p * np.log(xe) + q * np.log(one_minus)
            logf = logf - float(np.max(logf))
            w = logf - float(np.mean(logf))
            if np.all(np.isfinite(w)):
                starts.append(w)

    # Random Dirichlet seeds.
    for alpha in (0.1, 0.25, 0.5, 1.0, 2.0):
        f = rng.dirichlet(np.full(N, alpha))
        w = density_to_w(f)
        if w is not None:
            starts.append(w)

    # Sparse seeds.  These may be weak but can escape dense local minima.
    for frac in (0.05, 0.10, 0.20):
        m = max(2, min(N, int(round(N * frac))))
        f = np.full(N, 1e-10, dtype=np.float64)
        if m >= N:
            pos = np.arange(N)
        else:
            pos = rng.choice(N, size=m, replace=False)
        f[pos] = rng.exponential(1.0)
        w = density_to_w(f)
        if w is not None:
            starts.append(w)

    if len(starts) > 90:
        starts = starts[:90]
    return starts


def objective_and_grad(w):
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64)
    if w.size == 0 or not np.all(np.isfinite(w)):
        return (1e100, np.zeros_like(w))

    w = w - float(np.mean(w))
    a = softmax(w)
    n = a.size

    c = convolve_fft(a)
    c = np.where(c > 0.0, c, 0.0)
    cmax = float(np.max(c))
    beta = float(_GLOBAL_BETA)
    if beta <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(beta):
        beta = 1.0

    with np.errstate(over="ignore", under="ignore", invalid="ignore"):
        e = np.exp(beta * (c - cmax))
    e = np.where(np.isfinite(e), e, 0.0)
    Z = float(np.sum(e))
    if Z <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(Z):
        p = np.zeros(c.shape, dtype=np.float64)
        p[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
    else:
        p = e / Z

    lse = cmax + float(np.log(Z))
    val = 2.0 * n * lse + 1e-12 * float(np.dot(w, w))

    # Derivative of the smooth maximum with respect to a.
    # For convolution a*a, d c_k / d a_i = 2*a_{k-i} (with zeros outside range).
    # q_i = sum_j a_j p_{i+j} = sum_k p_k a_{k-i}.
    p_rev = p[::-1]
    need = max(1, 3 * n - 2)
    L = 1
    while L < need:
        L <<= 1
    AF = np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    PF = np.fft.rfft(p_rev, L)
    convq = np.fft.irfft(AF * PF, L)[:need]
    idx = (2 * n - 2) - np.arange(n)
    q = convq[idx]
    q = np.where(np.isfinite(q), q, 0.0)
    q = np.where(q > 0.0, q, 0.0)
    h = 2.0 * q

    total = float(np.dot(a, h))
    grad = 2.0 * n * a * (h - total)
    grad = grad + 2e-12 * w
    grad = np.where(np.isfinite(grad), grad, 0.0)
    return val, grad


def optimize_w(w0, time_limit):
    global _LOCAL_DEADLINE, _GLOBAL_BETA

    w = np.asarray(w0, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    if w.size == 0 or not np.all(np.isfinite(w)):
        return None, np.inf
    w = w - float(np.mean(w))
    a0 = softmax(w)
    best_w = w.copy()
    best_sc = fast_score(a0)

    if not _HAVE_SCIPY:
        return best_w, best_sc

    start = time.monotonic()
    local_deadline = min(start + max(1.0, float(time_limit)), _GLOBAL_DEADLINE)
    _LOCAL_DEADLINE = local_deadline
    n = w.size

    max_beta = max(20000.0, 40.0 * n)
    betas = [20.0, 100.0, 500.0, 2000.0, 10000.0, max_beta]
    uniq = []
    for b in betas:
        if not uniq or b > 1.25 * uniq[-1]:
            uniq.append(b)

    for beta in uniq:
        if time.monotonic() > local_deadline - 2.0:
            break
        _GLOBAL_BETA = float(beta)
        if n <= 64:
            maxiter = 90
        elif n <= 256:
            maxiter = 70
        elif n <= 1024:
            maxiter = 45
        else:
            maxiter = 30

        try:
            res = minimize(
                objective_and_grad,
                w,
                method="L-BFGS-B",
                jac=True,
                callback=_deadline_callback,
                options={
                    "maxiter": maxiter,
                    "ftol": 1e-7,
                    "gtol": 1e-4,
                    "maxls": 12,
                    "disp": False,
                },
            )
        except Exception:
            break

        if hasattr(res, "x") and res.x is not None:
            xn = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
            if xn.size == w.size and np.all(np.isfinite(xn)):
                w = xn - float(np.mean(xn))

        a = softmax(w)
        sc = fast_score(a)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best_w = w.copy()

    _LOCAL_DEADLINE = _GLOBAL_DEADLINE
    return best_w, best_sc


def add_to_pool(pool, a, score, max_size=6):
    global _POOL_COUNTER
    if a is None or score is None:
        return pool
    score = float(score)
    if not np.isfinite(score):
        return pool
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == 0 or not np.all(np.isfinite(a)):
        return pool
    _POOL_COUNTER += 1
    pool.append((score, _POOL_COUNTER, a.copy()))
    pool.sort()
    if len(pool) > max_size:
        pool = pool[:max_size]
    return pool


def consider_candidate(a, best_seq, best_exact, best_fast_seq, best_fast, do_exact=False):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == 0:
        return best_seq, best_exact, best_fast_seq, best_fast
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 0.01:
        return best_seq, best_exact, best_fast_seq, best_fast
    a = a / s

    sc_f = fast_score(a)
    if not np.isfinite(sc_f):
        return best_seq, best_exact, best_fast_seq, best_fast

    if sc_f < best_fast:
        best_fast = sc_f
        best_fast_seq = a.copy()

    if do_exact and a.size <= 4000:
        try:
            sc_e = float(evaluate_sequence(a.tolist()))
        except Exception:
            sc_e = np.inf
        if np.isfinite(sc_e) and sc_e < best_exact:
            best_exact = sc_e
            best_seq = a.copy()

    return best_seq, best_exact, best_fast_seq, best_fast


def local_search(a, rng, time_limit):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 0.0:
        return a, np.inf
    a = a / s

    n = a.size
    best = a.copy()
    best_sc = fast_score(best)
    if not np.isfinite(best_sc):
        return best, np.inf

    deadline = time.monotonic() + max(1.0, float(time_limit))
    sigma = 0.15
    t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        cand = best.copy()
        r = float(rng.random())

        if r < 0.42:
            lo = max(1, n // 400) if n > 12 else 1
            hi = max(lo + 1, n // 12)
            hi = min(n - 1, hi)
            if hi > lo:
                width = int(rng.integers(lo, hi + 1))
                start = int(rng.integers(0, n - width + 1))
                factor = float(np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, sigma)))
                cand[start:start + width] *= factor
            else:
                idx = int(rng.integers(0, n))
                cand[idx] *= float(np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, sigma)))

        elif r < 0.56:
            slope = float(rng.normal(0.0, 0.9))
            cand *= np.exp(slope * t)

        elif r < 0.72:
            amp = float(rng.normal(0.0, 0.35))
            freq = float(rng.uniform(1.0, 4.0))
            phase = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
            cand *= np.exp(amp * np.sin(freq * np.pi * t + phase))

        else:
            i = int(rng.integers(0, n))
            j = int(rng.integers(0, n))
            if i != j:
                frac = float(rng.uniform(-0.35, 0.35))
                move = frac * best[i]
                if best[i] - move > 0.0:
                    cand[i] -= move
                    cand[j] += move

        cand = np.maximum(cand, 0.0)
        cs = float(cand.sum())
        if cs <= 0.0:
            continue
        cand = cand / cs

        sc = fast_score(cand)
        if sc < best_sc - 1e-13:
            best = cand
            best_sc = sc
            sigma = max(0.015, sigma * 0.995)
        elif rng.random() < 0.002 and np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc + 1e-5:
            best = cand
            best_sc = sc

    return best, best_sc


def gradient_polish(a, time_limit):
    global _GLOBAL_BETA

    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 0.0:
        return a, np.inf
    a = a / s

    n = a.size
    w = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-300))
    w = w - float(np.mean(w))

    best = a.copy()
    best_sc = fast_score(a)
    curr = a.copy()
    curr_sc = best_sc

    deadline = time.monotonic() + max(1.0, float(time_limit))
    lr = 0.01
    stall = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline and lr > 1e-6:
        _GLOBAL_BETA = max(3000.0, 30.0 * n)
        try:
            _, grad = objective_and_grad(w)
        except Exception:
            break

        grad = np.asarray(grad, dtype=np.float64)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)):
            break
        gn = float(np.linalg.norm(grad))
        if gn < 1e-12:
            break
        g = grad / gn

        improved = False
        for step in (lr, lr * 0.5, lr * 0.2, 1e-4):
            w_trial = w - step * g
            a_trial = softmax(w_trial)
            sc_trial = fast_score(a_trial)
            if sc_trial < curr_sc - 1e-13:
                w = w_trial
                curr = a_trial
                curr_sc = sc_trial
                improved = True
                break

        if improved:
            if curr_sc < best_sc:
                best_sc = curr_sc
                best = curr.copy()
            stall = 0
            lr = min(0.05, lr * 1.15)
        else:
            stall += 1
            lr *= 0.6
            if stall > 6:
                break

    _GLOBAL_BETA = 1.0
    return best, best_sc


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    global _GLOBAL_DEADLINE

    start_time = time.monotonic()
    try:
        budget = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        budget = 2400.0
    budget = max(1.0, min(budget, 2400.0))
    _GLOBAL_DEADLINE = start_time + max(1.0, budget - 8.0)

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed if seed is not None else 42)

    best_seq = None
    best_exact = np.inf
    best_fast_seq = None
    best_fast = np.inf
    pool = []

    base_a = None
    hs = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
    if hs is not None:
        try:
            arr = np.asarray(hs, dtype=np.float64)
            if arr.ndim == 1 and arr.size > 0 and np.all(np.isfinite(arr)):
                arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
                s = float(arr.sum())
                if s > 0.01:
                    arr = arr / s
                    base_a = arr
                    best_seq, best_exact, best_fast_seq, best_fast = consider_candidate(
                        arr, best_seq, best_exact, best_fast_seq, best_fast, do_exact=True
                    )
        except Exception:
            base_a = None

    # Quick evaluation of deterministic/random parametric seeds.  This usually
    # already finds a solid asymmetric sequence with score well below 2.
    quick_ns = [8, 16, 32, 64, 128, 256, 512]
    for N in quick_ns:
        if time.monotonic() > _GLOBAL_DEADLINE - 90.0:
            break
        for w in family_starts(N, rng):
            a = softmax(w)
            sc = fast_score(a)
            if np.isfinite(sc):
                if sc < best_fast:
                    best_fast = sc
                    best_fast_seq = a.copy()
                if sc < 2.0 - 1e-9:
                    pool = add_to_pool(pool, a, sc, max_size=6)

    if best_fast_seq is not None:
        best_seq, best_exact, best_fast_seq, best_fast = consider_candidate(
            best_fast_seq, best_seq, best_exact, best_fast_seq, best_fast, do_exact=True
        )

    # Full nonlinear optimization stages.  The plan balances small, medium and
    # larger lengths.  Smaller lengths are easier to optimize; larger lengths
    # may approximate the continuum optimum better.
    plans = [
        (32, 3, 25),
        (64, 4, 35),
        (128, 5, 50),
        (256, 5, 80),
        (512, 4, 90),
        (1024, 3, 80),
    ]

    for N, num_starts, sec_per in plans:
        if time.monotonic() > _GLOBAL_DEADLINE - 60.0:
            break
        remaining = _GLOBAL_DEADLINE - time.monotonic() - 45.0
        if remaining <= 20.0:
            break

        starts = family_starts(N, rng)

        if base_a is not None:
            wb = density_to_w(resize_a(base_a, N))
            if wb is not None:
                starts.append(wb)

        if best_fast_seq is not None:
            wbf = density_to_w(resize_a(best_fast_seq, N))
            if wbf is not None:
                starts.append(wbf)

        scored = []
        for w in starts:
            a = softmax(w)
            sc = fast_score(a)
            if np.isfinite(sc):
                scored.append((sc, w))
                if sc < best_fast:
                    best_fast = sc
                    best_fast_seq = a.copy()

        scored.sort()
        if not scored:
            if best_fast_seq is not None:
                aw = resize_a(best_fast_seq, N)
                zw = density_to_w(aw)
                if zw is not None:
                    scored.append((best_fast, zw))
            else:
                continue

        scored = scored[:max(1, num_starts)]

        tleft = _GLOBAL_DEADLINE - time.monotonic() - 45.0
        per = max(8.0, min(float(sec_per), tleft / max(1.0, len(scored))))

        for sc0, w0 in scored:
            if time.monotonic() > _GLOBAL_DEADLINE - 40.0:
                break
            tper = max(8.0, min(float(sec_per), ( _GLOBAL_DEADLINE - time.monotonic() - 40.0) / max(1.0, len(scored))))
            w_opt, sc_opt = optimize_w(w0, tper)
            if w_opt is None:
                continue
            a_opt = softmax(w_opt)
            if not (np.isfinite(sc_opt) and sc_opt < best_fast + 0.05):
                sc_opt = fast_score(a_opt)

            best_seq, best_exact, best_fast_seq, best_fast = consider_candidate(
                a_opt, best_seq, best_exact, best_fast_seq, best_fast, do_exact=True
            )
            if sc_opt < best_fast + 0.03:
                pool = add_to_pool(pool, a_opt, sc_opt, max_size=8)

    # If time remains, try one or two long lengths using the best shape found so
    # far as a continuum-inspired seed.
    if time.monotonic() < _GLOBAL_DEADLINE - 120.0 and best_fast_seq is not None:
        for N in (1536, 2048):
            if time.monotonic() > _GLOBAL_DEADLINE - 90.0:
                break
            tleft = _GLOBAL_DEADLINE - time.monotonic() - 60.0
            if tleft < 80.0:
                break
            a0 = resize_a(best_fast_seq, N)
            w0 = density_to_w(a0)
            if w0 is None:
                continue
            w_opt, sc_opt = optimize_w(w0, max(30.0, min(220.0, tleft - 50.0)))
            if w_opt is None:
                continue
            a_opt = softmax(w_opt)
            best_seq, best_exact, best_fast_seq, best_fast = consider_candidate(
                a_opt, best_seq, best_exact, best_fast_seq, best_fast, do_exact=True
            )
            sc_now = fast_score(a_opt)
            if np.isfinite(sc_now) and sc_now < best_fast + 0.03:
                pool = add_to_pool(pool, a_opt, sc_now, max_size=8)

    # Final polishing of the best few candidates.
    if pool:
        remaining = _GLOBAL_DEADLINE - time.monotonic() - 12.0
        if remaining > 15.0:
            top = pool[:3]
            per = max(5.0, remaining / max(1, len(top)))
            for sc0, _, a0 in top:
                if time.monotonic() > _GLOBAL_DEADLINE - 10.0:
                    break
                tleft = _GLOBAL_DEADLINE - time.monotonic() - 10.0
                if tleft < 5.0:
                    break
                t_local = min(per, max(5.0, tleft * 0.55))
                t_grad = max(4.0, min(60.0, tleft - t_local))
                a1, sc1 = local_search(a0, rng, t_local)
                a2, sc2 = gradient_polish(a1, t_grad)
                if sc2 < sc1:
                    a1, sc1 = a2, sc2
                best_seq, best_exact, best_fast_seq, best_fast = consider_candidate(
                    a1, best_seq, best_exact, best_fast_seq, best_fast, do_exact=True
                )
                if np.isfinite(sc1) and sc1 < best_fast + 0.05:
                    pool = add_to_pool(pool, a1, sc1, max_size=8)

    # Final exact check of the best fast-score candidate.
    if best_fast_seq is not None:
        best_seq, best_exact, best_fast_seq, best_fast = consider_candidate(
            best_fast_seq, best_seq, best_exact, best_fast_seq, best_fast, do_exact=True
        )

    if best_seq is None and base_a is not None:
        best_seq = base_a

    if best_seq is None and best_fast_seq is not None:
        best_seq = best_fast_seq

    if best_seq is None:
        best_seq = np.full(64, 1.0, dtype=np.float64)

    out = np.asarray(best_seq, dtype=np.float64).flatten()
    out = out[np.isfinite(out)]
    out = np.clip(out, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(out.sum())
    if s <= 0.01:
        out = np.full(64, 1.0, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        out = out / s

    return [float(x) for x in out]
```
